Remarks: Figures for the year ended 31 March 2024 are shown in brackets
註：       截至2024年3月31日止年度之數字於括號內顯示
1
   Sales = Revenue – Licensing Income
   銷售 = 收入 – 品牌業務收入
2
   Gross Profit = Consolidated Gross Profit – Gross Profit of Licensing Income
   毛利 = 綜合毛利 – 品牌業務收入毛利
3
   Gold & Platinum refers to gold & platinum products sold by weight basing on international market price, i.e. at non-fixed price
   黃金及鉑金是根據國際市場價格按重量出售的黃金及鉑金產品（即非定價）
4
   Same store sales (“SSS”) represented a comparison of sales of the same self-operated shop having full day operations in the comparable periods and such
   data did not include sales of licensed shops and Mainland’s e-commerce business
   同店銷售（     「同店銷售」        ）為同一間自營店於可比較期內有完整日營運的銷售額比較，有關數據並不包括品牌店及內地電子商務業務的銷售額

     Net (borrowing)/cash represent bank borrowings and gold loans less cash and bank balances (including structural bank deposit, that classified as derivative
     financial instrument).
     淨（貸款）╱現金為銀行貸款加黃金借款減銀行及現金結餘（包括分類為衍生金融工具的結構性銀行存款）                                                                。
4
     (Long-term Liabilities + Current Liabilities) ÷ Capital and Reserves Attributable to Equity Holders of the Company
    （長期負債 + 流動負債）÷ 本公司權益持有人應佔資本及儲備
5
     Profit Attributable to Equity Holders of the Company ÷ Capital and Reserves Attributable to Equity Holders of the Company
     本公司權益持有人應佔溢利 ÷ 本公司權益持有人應佔資本及儲備
6
     Profit Attributable to Equity Holders of the Company ÷ (Long-term Assets + Current Assets)
     本公司權益持有人應佔溢利 ÷（長期資產 + 流動資產）

GOLDSTYLE
Goldstyle is crafted with pioneering gold-crafting techniques to
nearly double up the hardness of gold jewellery compared to that
of conventional gold, to create a variety of gold jewellery that rivals
the design quality of karat gold. Building on the foundation of
Goldstyle, the Group introduced Goldstyle • X featuring a modern
aesthetic blend of gold and diamond, adding on-trend sparkle to
gold products.

Moreover, Goldstyle created an array of gold jewellery designs
featuring the vibrant gourd motifs with distinctive carved patterns
that are suitable for everyday wear and expressing personal
style. The brand also invited “Share Love and Fun Ambassadors”
Guo Junchen and Bi Wenjun to showcase these pieces at
events, and collaborated with actress Zhang Ruonan on product
photoshoots, unveiling gold’s contemporary appeal. In future,
the brand will continue to incorporate diverse elements into gold
jewellery designs, offering customers a wider choice of stylish and
fashionable products.

CHAIRMAN AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE OFFICER                                  主席及行政總裁
The Chairman leads the Board in the formulation of strategies and     主 席 領 導 董 事 會 制 定 策 略 及 達 成 目 標，並 確
in the achievement of objectives and ensures that all directors       保全體董事對董事會會議上的事項獲適當的
are properly briefed on issues arising at the Board meetings          簡介，並及時獲得足夠、完整及可靠的信息。
and receive adequate, complete and reliable information in a          行政總裁獲授權力，負責本公司業務營運、推
timely manner. The Chief Executive Officer is delegated with the      行本公司之策略，以實踐業務目標。主席及行
authority and responsible for running the Company’s business,         政總裁兩者的職務現時均由執行董事黃偉常
implementing the Company’s strategies in achieving business           先生出任。董事會相信，以現時的管治架構而
objectives. Both the Chairman and the Chief Executive Officer         言（由一人兼任主席及行政總裁，以及大部分
positions are currently held by Mr. WONG Wai Sheung, the              均為非執行董事）足以有效使本公司管理層達
executive director. The Board believes that the current governance    致均衡權責，符合本公司現時的最佳利益。
structure, with one person serving as both the Chairman and
the Chief Executive Officer and the majority being non-executive
directors, provides an effective balance of power and authority for
the management of the Company and is in the best interest of the
Company at the present stage.

NON-EXECUTIVE DIRECTORS                                               非執行董事
All the non-executive directors are appointed for a term of not       所 有 非 執 行 董 事 之 任 期 為 不 超 過 兩 年，而 其
more than two years, and the appointments are renewable from          任 期 可 不 時 重 續，惟 須 於 股 東 週 年 大 會 上 輪
time to time, subject to retirement by rotation at AGM.               值告退。

BOARD COMMITTEES                                                      董事委員會
The Board has established three Board committees, including           董 事 會 轄 下 設 有 三 個 董 事 委 員 會，包 括 審 核
the Audit Committee, the Nomination Committee and the                 委員會、提名委員會及薪酬委員會，以監督本
Remuneration Committee, for overseeing the respective aspects         集團各有關範疇事務。
of the Group’s affairs.

The Board committees are provided with sufficient resources to        董事會轄下委員會均獲提供充裕資源以履行
discharge their duties and, upon reasonable request, are able to      其職責，並可於提出合理要求後，在適當情況
seek independent professional advice at the Company’s expense         下尋求獨立專業意見，費用由本公司承擔。
in appropriate circumstances.

(i)   Audit Committee                                                 (i)   審核委員會
      The Audit Committee was established on 1 February 1999                本 公 司 於1999年2月1日 成 立 審 核 委 員
      with written terms of reference accessible on the websites            會，其書面職權範圍載於本公司及香港

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                    風險管理及內部監控
The Board is responsible for the Company’s systems of risk              董事會負責本公司風險管理及內部監控制
management and internal control and for reviewing their                 度，並透過審核委員會每年檢討其成效。
effectiveness annually through the Audit Committee.

The risk management and internal control systems are aligned            風 險 管 理 及 內 部 監 控 制 度 結 合C o m m i t t e e
with the Internal Control Integrated Framework of the Committee         of Sponsoring Organisations of the Treadway
of Sponsoring Organisations of the Treadway Commission                  Commission（「 COSO 」）的內部監控綜合框
(“COSO”), which comprises 17 principles under 5 components,             架，該綜合框架由5個部份分17項原則組成，5
including Control Environment, Risk Assessment, Control Activities,     個部份包括控制環境、風險評估、控制活動、
Information and Communication and Monitoring Activities.                訊息與溝通及監控活動。

The risk management and internal control systems are designed           風險管理及內部監控制度旨在管理而非消除
to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve          未 能 達 成 業 務 目 標 的 風 險，而 且 只 能 就 不 會
business objectives, and can only provide reasonable but not            有重大的失實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對
absolute assurance against material misstatement or loss.               的保證。

internal control evaluation reports submitted by the Internal Audit     理 及 內 部 監 控 評 估 報 告。該 等 報 告 概 述 下 列
Department. The reports summarised information relating to the          工作之詳情：

•    the results of selective testing of internal control procedures,   •    對本公司內部監控程序、資產及財務記
     assets, and financial records of the Company;                           錄之抽樣調查結果；

     systems installed by the Company; and                                   之一般評估；及

subsidiaries are principally engaged in the sourcing, designing,         要從事各類黃鉑金首飾及珠寶首飾產品之採

Principal Risks and Uncertainties                                      主要風險及不明朗因素
The Board is ultimately responsible for ensuring that the risk         董事會最終負責確保本集團的風險管理實踐
management practices of the Group are sufficient to mitigate           能充份而有效地減低業務及營運中的風險。
the risks present in our businesses and operations as efficiently      董事會將部份職責委派予各個部門。
and effectively as possible. The Board delegates some of such
responsibilities to various departments.

The Group’s financial position, operations, business and               本集團的財務狀況、營運、業務及前景會受以
prospects may be affected by the following identified risks and        下 已 識 別 風 險 及 不 確 定 因 素 影 響。本 集 團 採
uncertainties. The Group adopts risk management policies,              用風險管理政策、措施及監控系統，防範及控
measures and monitoring systems to pre-empt and contain                制所面臨的相應已識別風險。
exposures associated with the identified risks.

Operational Risks                                                      經營風險
Rent                                                                   租金
As a retailer, rent accounts for a considerable portion of the         作為零售商，租金佔本集團的開支比重不少，
Group’s expenses. Rental increment on retail shops directly            零售商舖的租金上升直接影響本集團之盈
affects the profit of the Group. The Group strikes a good balance      利。本 集 團 在 店 舖 擴 張 與 增 加 盈 利 之 間 取 得
between shop expansion and profit increment. The Group strives         一 個 良 好 平 衡。本 集 團 積 極 透 過 與 業 主 商 討
to lower rent via negotiations with landlords, closure of branches     減租、關閉盈利能力較低的店舖，並於其他地
with low profitability and searching for suitable locations in other   段尋求合適位置開設新店。
areas for new shops.

Prices of Raw Materials                                                原材料價格
Fluctuations in prices of raw materials may adversely affect our       原材料價格波動對我們的盈利能力或有不良
profitability. We strive to improve our purchasing procedures in       影響。我們致力改善採購程序，務求將原材料
order to maintain reasonable raw materials costs. On the other         成本維持在合理水平。另一方面，本集團的黃
hand, the Group has a gold hedging policy with a ratio of around       金對沖政策為約25-30%的比率，旨在於金價
25-30% which mitigates the potential adverse impacts on gross          出 現 顯 著 波 動 時，減 低 對 毛 利 率 及 整 體 財 務
profit margins and overall financial performance arising from          表 現 之 潛 在 影 響。管 理 層 定 期 檢 視 對 沖 策 略
pronounced volatility in gold prices. Management regularly reviews     之 成 效 及 覆 蓋 範 圍，並 將 視 乎 市 場 情 況 作 出
the effectiveness and coverage of the hedging strategy and will        適當調整。
make appropriate adjustments in response to market conditions.

promptly to changes in regulatory requirements, or to implement       及 恐 怖 主 義 融 資 的 措 施，甚 至 在 不 知 情 情 況
effective measures across operating regions for preventing money      下涉及違規行為，將可能引致法律責任、聲譽
laundering and terrorist financing, may result in inadvertent non-    損害及業務中斷風險等重大風險。
compliance. Such incidents could expose the Group to significant

in relevant laws and regulations and make timely adjustments to       運持續符合法律及道德標準，維持企業誠信、

with legal and ethical standards, maintaining corporate integrity,

guard against risks such as phishing emails, ransomware, identity        侵 等 風 險，可 能 導 致 敏 感 資 料 外 洩、業 務 中
theft, and system intrusions may result in the leakage of sensitive      斷、財務損失，甚至未能符合日益嚴格之數據
data, business disruption, financial losses, and non-compliance          私隱法規，從而引發監管罰則、法律爭議及集
with increasingly stringent data privacy regulations. These              團 聲 譽 受 損 等 後 果。管 理 層 已 推 行 多 項 網 絡
consequences could lead to regulatory penalties, legal disputes,         安全措施，包括加強員工培訓、定期進行滲透
and damage to the Group’s reputation. The management has                 測 試、採 用 多 重 認 證 機 制 及 強 化 雲 端 平 台 防
implemented various cybersecurity measures, including enhanced           禦能力，以提升整體資訊保安水平。
employee training, regular penetration testing, adoption of multi-
factor authentication mechanisms, and reinforcement of cloud

The emolument policy regarding the employees of the Group                 本集團僱員之薪酬政策乃基於僱員之表現、
is based on their merit, qualifications and competence. The               資 歷 及 能 力。董 事 之 薪 酬 由 薪 酬 委 員 會 經 參

the Remuneration Committee, having regard to the Company’s                比 較 市 場 數 據 而 檢 討 及 釐 定。概 無 任 何 董 事

statistics. No director, or any of his or her associates is involved in
deciding his or her own remuneration.

請參閱綜合財務報表附註4(a)及23。

於2025年3月31日， 貴集團存貨的賬面值為10,739百
萬港元。 貴集團存貨主要包括零售店舖、生產設施及
倉庫的黃金、鉑金及珠寶首飾產品。存貨乃按成本值與
可變現淨值兩者之較低者估值。存貨的可變現淨值為
日常業務過程中的估計售價減去完成的估計成本及銷
售開支。存貨的賬面值絕大部分為貴金屬及珠寶成本，
而該等成本承受市值變動風險。存貨的可變現淨值按
管理層就（其中包括）黃金、鉑金及珠寶首飾產品的真
實性及價值、珠寶首飾產品的過往銷售表現以及黃金、
鉑金及珠寶首飾產品所用貴金屬及珠寶的估計售價的
估計及判斷進行評估。管理層委聘獨立寶石鑑定師協
助評估所選珠寶首飾產品的真實性及價值。此外，個別
存貨的內在價值高昂兼具便攜性質，令其存在較高的
遭偷盜風險。

1     GENERAL INFORMATION                                              1   一般資料
      Luk Fook Holdings (International) Limited (the “Company”)            六 福 集 團（國 際）有 限 公 司（「本 公 司」）
      was incorporated in Bermuda on 3 September 1996 as                   於1996年9月3日 在 百 慕 達 根 據 百 慕 達
      a company with limited liability under the Companies Act             公 司 法 註 冊 成 立 為 有 限 公 司，其 註 冊
      of Bermuda. The address of its registered office is Victoria         辦事處地址為Victoria Place, 5th Floor
      Place, 5th Floor 31 Victoria Street, Hamilton HM10,                  31 Victoria Street, Hamilton HM10,
      Bermuda.                                                             Bermuda。

      The Company and its subsidiaries (together, the “Group”)             本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）主
      are principally engaged in the sourcing, designing,                  要從事各類黃鉑金首飾及珠寶首飾之採
      wholesaling, trademark licensing and retailing of a variety of       購、設計、批發、商標授權及零售業務。
      gold and platinum jewellery, and gem-set jewellery.

      The Company’s shares were listed on the main board of                本公司股份於1997年5月6日在香港聯合
      The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock                  交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上市。
      Exchange”) on 6 May 1997. 3DG Holdings (International)               金至尊集團（國際）有限公司（       「金至尊」  ，
      Limited (“3DG”, together with its subsidiaries collectively          連同其附屬公司統稱「金至尊集團」，前
      referred as “3DG Group”, formerly known as Hong Kong                 稱香港資源控股有限公司）為本公司附
      Resources Holdings Company Limited) are subsidiaries of              屬 公 司，並 於 聯 交 所 主 板 上 市（股 份 代
      the Company, and listed on the main board of the Stock               號：2882）。
      Exchange with Stock Code 2882.

      These consolidated financial statements are presented in             除另有說明者外，此等綜合財務報表均

      The principal accounting policies applied in the preparation         編製此等綜合財務報表時所應用之主要

      been prepared in accordance with HKFRS Accounting                    師公會（「香港會計師公會」）頒佈的《香
      Standards as issued by the Hong Kong Institute of                    港財務報告會計準則 》及香港公司條例

      Cap.622.

      (including derivative financial instruments), and gold loans         表按歷史成本慣例編製。

      (c)   Capital risk management                                         (c)   資本風險管理
            The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to                   本集團管理資本的目標為保障本
            safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going                  集團持續運作的能力，從而為股東
            concern in order to provide returns for shareholders                  提供回報、為其他利益相關者提供
            and benefits for other stakeholders and to maintain an                利益以及保持理想的資本架構以
            optimal capital structure to reduce the cost of capital.              減低資本成本。

            The Group manages the capital structure and makes                     本集團管理資本架構，並因應經濟
            adjustments to it in the light of changes in economic                 環境的變動作出調整。為保持或調
            conditions. In order to maintain or adjust the capital                整資本架構，本集團可調整支付予
            structure, the Group may adjust the dividend                          股東的股息、發行新股或獲得新的
            payments to shareholders, issue of new shares or                      銀行貸款。
            obtain new bank borrowings.

            The Group also monitors capital on the basis of                       本集團亦以負債資產比率監察其
            gearing ratio. The gearing ratio is calculated as                     資 本。負 債 資 產 比 率 是 以 淨 債 項
            net debt divided by total capital. Net debt/(cash)                    除以總資本計算。淨債項／（現金）
            is calculated as total borrowings (including bank                     乃按貸款總額（包括銀行貸款及黃
            borrowings and gold loans) less cash and bank                         金借貸）減現金及銀行結餘計算得
            balances. Total capital is calculated as “equity”, as                 出。總資本乃以「權益」     （如綜合資
            shown in the consolidated balance sheet, plus net                     產負債表所示）加淨債項／（現金）
            debt/(cash).                                                          計算得出。

4   CRITICAL ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES AND                                4   關鍵會計估計及判斷
    JUDGEMENTS
    Estimates and judgements used in preparing the                       在編製綜合財務報表時所採用之估計及
    consolidated financial statements are continually evaluated          判 斷，乃 按 過 往 經 驗 及 其 他 因 素（包 括
    and are based on historical experience and other factors,            根據不同情況對未來事件作出之合理預
    including expectations of future events that are believed            計）不斷作出評估。所得會計估算按其定
    to be reasonable under the circumstances. The resulting              義，甚 少 會 與 相 關 實 際 結 果 相 等。下 文
    accounting estimates will, by definition, seldom equal the           討論有重大風險導致下一財政年度資產
    related actual results. The estimates and assumptions that           及負債之賬面值須作出重大調整之估計
    have a significant risk of causing a material adjustment to          及假設。
    the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities within the next
    financial year are set out below.

    (a)   Net realisable value of inventories                            (a)   存貨可變現淨值
          Net realisable value of inventories is the estimated                 存貨可變現淨值為日常業務中的
          selling price in the ordinary course of business,                    估計售價扣除估計完工成本及銷
          less estimated costs of completion and selling                       售開支後的數額。此等估計乃根據
          expenses. These estimates are based on the current                   現時市況及生產與出售類似性質
          market condition and the historical experience of                    產品的過往經驗而作出，並可因顧
          manufacturing and selling products of similar nature.                客喜好的轉變及競爭對手應對嚴
          It could change significantly as a result of changes in              峻的行業週期所作的行動而有重
          customer taste and competitor actions in response to                 大差異。管理層於各結算日重新評
          severe industry cycle. Management reassesses these                   估此等估計。
          estimates at each balance sheet date.

      (a) Sales of goods – wholesale                                     (a)   銷售貨品－批發
           The Group manufactures and sells a range of gold                    本集團製造及於批發市場上銷售
           jewellery and gem-set of jewellery products in the                  一系列黃金首飾及珠寶首飾產品。
           wholesale market. Sales are recognised when control                 當產品控制權已轉移時（即當產品
           of the products has transferred, being when the                     交付予品牌商╱批發商，品牌商╱
           products are delivered to the licensees/wholesalers,                批發商對銷售產品的渠道及價格
           the licensees/wholesalers have full discretion over the             擁有全面酌情權，且並無未履行責
           channel and price to sell the products and there is no              任可能影響對產品的接收時），銷
           unfulfilled obligation that could affect the acceptance             售額乃予以確認。當產品已付運至
           of the products. Delivery does not occur until the                  指定地點，廢棄及損失風險已轉讓
           products have been delivered to the specified location,             予品牌商╱批發商，而品牌商╱批
           the risks of obsolescence and loss have been                        發商已根據銷售合約接收產品、接
           transferred to the licensees/wholesalers, and either                收條款已作廢，或本集團有客觀證
           the licensees/wholesalers have accepted the products                據證明已滿足所有產品接收條件
           in accordance with sales contract, the acceptance                   後，方始計作已交付貨品。
           provisions have lapsed, or the Group has objective
           evidence that all criteria for acceptance have been
           satisfied.

           A sales refund liability and an asset for the right of              銷售退貨權的銷售退款負債及資
           return goods are recognised in the consolidated                     產乃於綜合資產負債表中確認。本
           balance sheet. The Group uses the expected value                    集團採用預期價值法估計信貸退
           method to estimate the credit refund or return which                款或退貨，其為本集團有權獲得的
           best predicts the amount of variable consideration to               可變代價金額之最佳預測。
           which the Group entitled.

           A contract liability is also recognised when the                    當品牌商╱批發商於本集團將產
           licensees/wholesalers pay deposits before the Group                 品的控制權轉移予品牌商╱批發
           transfers control of the products to the licensees/                 商前支付按金時，亦會確認合約負
           wholesalers.                                                        債。

10 INCOME TAX EXPENSES (Continued)                                  10 所得稅開支（續）
   Accounting policies for current and deferred                       即期及遞延所得稅的會計政策
   income tax
   The tax expense for the period comprises current and               期內稅項開支包括即期及遞延稅項。稅
   deferred tax. Tax is recognised in the consolidated income         項於綜合損益表確認，惟倘稅項與於其
   statement, except to the extent that it relates to items           他全面收入確認或直接於權益確認之項
   recognised in other comprehensive income or directly               目 有 關 者 除 外。在 該 情 況 下，稅 項 亦 會
   in equity. In this case the tax is also recognised in other        分別於其他全面收入或直接於權益內確
   comprehensive income or directly in equity, respectively.          認。

   The tax expense or credit for the period is the tax payable        期內稅項開支或抵免指就當期應課稅收
   on the current period’s taxable income based on the                入按各司法權區之適用所得稅率計算的
   applicable income tax rate for each jurisdiction adjusted by       應付稅項，並就暫時差額及未動用稅項
   changes in deferred tax assets and liabilities attributable to     虧損的相關遞延稅項資產及負債變動作
   temporary differences and to unused tax losses.                    出調整。

   (a)   Current income tax                                           (a)   即期所得稅
         The current income tax charge is calculated on the                 即期所得稅開支乃按報告期末在
         basis of the tax laws enacted or substantively enacted             本公司及其附屬公司經營及產生
         at the end of the reporting period in the countries                應課稅收入之國家已頒佈或實質
         where the Company and its subsidiaries operate and                 頒佈之稅法計算。管理層定期評估
         generate taxable income. Management periodically                   就適用稅項法規有待詮釋之情況
         evaluates positions taken in tax returns with respect to           之稅項申報所採取措施，並考慮稅
         situations in which applicable tax regulation is subject           務機關是否有可能接受不確定稅
         to interpretation and considers whether it is probable             項處理。本集團根據最可能出現金
         that a taxation authority will accept an uncertain tax             額或預期價值（視乎何種方法能更
         treatment. The Group measures its tax balances either              準確預測不確定因素的解決方案
         based on the most likely amount or the expected                    而定）計量其稅項結餘。
         value, depending on which method provides a better
         prediction of the resolution of the uncertainty.

      Accounting policy for property, plant and                                           物業、廠房及設備的會計政策
      equipment
      Property, plant and equipment are stated at historical cost                         物業、廠房及設備乃按歷史成本減折舊
      less depreciation. Historical cost includes expenditure that is                     列賬。歷史成本包括收購該等項目直接
      directly attributable to the acquisition of the items.                              應佔之開支。

      Subsequent costs are included in the asset’s carrying                               僅於與項目相關之日後經濟利益可能流
      amount or recognised as a separate asset, as appropriate,                           入本集團，且該項目之成本能夠可靠計
      only when it is probable that future economic benefits                              量時，其後成本方會計入該項資產之賬
      associated with the item will flow to the Group and the cost                        面值或確認為一項獨立資產（視適用情
      of the item can be measured reliably. The carrying amount of                        況而定）。重置部分之賬面值於重置時終
      the replaced part is derecognised when replaced. All other                          止確認。所有其他維修及保養會於其產
      repairs and maintenance are charged to the consolidated                             生之財務期間內在綜合損益表支銷。
      income statement during the financial period in which they
      are incurred.

   Construction in progress represents property, plant and              在建工程指在建設中或待安裝的物業、
   equipment under construction or pending installation and is          廠 房 及 設 備，並 按 成 本 減 減 值 虧 損（如

   completion, the relevant assets are transferred to property,         廠房及設備。
   plant and equipment at fair value or cost less accumulated
   impairment losses.

   An asset’s carrying amount is written down immediately               倘資產之賬面值高於其估計可收回金
   to its recoverable amount if the asset’s carrying amount is          額，則該項資產之賬面值會即時撇減至
   greater than its estimated recoverable amount.                       其可收回金額。

   Gains and losses on disposals are determined by comparing            出售盈虧透過將所得款項淨額與有關資
   net proceeds with carrying amount of the relevant assets             產之賬面值比較釐定並計入綜合損益
   and are included in the consolidated income statement.               表。

16 INVESTMENT PROPERTIES (Continued)                                   16 投資物業（續）
   Accounting policy for investment properties                           投資物業的會計政策
   Investment property, principally comprising leasehold land            投資物業主要由租賃土地及樓宇所組
   and buildings, is held for long-term rental yields or for capital     成，為獲得長期租金收益或資本升值或
   appreciation or both, and is not occupied by the Group.               兩 者 兼 備 而 持 有，且 並 非 由 本 集 團 佔
                                                                         用。

   Investment property is measured at cost, including related            投資物業按成本值（包括相關交易成本）
   transaction costs, less accumulated depreciation and                  減 累 計 折 舊 及 累 計 減 值 虧 損（如 有）列
   accumulated impairment losses, if any.                                賬。

   Depreciation is provided using the straight-line method               折 舊 採 用 直 線 法 於 其 為 期30至50年 之
   to write off the cost of the investment properties over               估計可使用年期撇銷投資物業之成本。
   their estimated useful lives of 30 to 50 years. Where the             倘投資物業賬面值高於其估計可收回金
   carrying amount of an investment property is greater                  額，則即時撇減至其可收回金額。
   than its estimated recoverable amount, it is written down
   immediately to its recoverable amount.

   Subsequent expenditure is charged to the asset’s carrying             僅於與項目相關之日後經濟利益可能
   amount only when it is probable that future economic                  流入本集團，且該項目之成本能夠可靠
   benefits associated with the item will flow to the Group and          計量時，方會自資產賬面值扣除其後開
   the cost of the item can be measured reliably. All other repair       支。所有其他維修及保養成本於產生之
   and maintenance costs are expensed in the consolidated                財政期間在綜合損益表支銷。
   income statement during the financial period in which they
   are incurred.

   If an investment property becomes owner-occupied, it is               倘投資物業由業主自用，則重新分類為
   reclassified as property, plant and equipment. At the date of         物 業、廠 房 及 設 備。於 重 新 分 類 當 日，
   reclassification, its cost and accumulated depreciation are           其成本與累計折舊乃轉撥至物業、廠房
   transferred to property, plant and equipment and become               及設備，並按照會計處理成為其成本與
   its cost and accumulated depreciation for accounting                  累計折舊。倘業主自用物業因改變用途
   purposes. If an owner-occupied property becomes an                    而成為投資物業，則重新分類為投資物
   investment property because its use has changed, it is                業。
   reclassified as investment property.

17 LEASES (Continued)                                                   17 租賃（續）
      Accounting policy for leases                                        租賃的會計政策
      Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a                 租賃在租賃資產可供本集團使用之日確
      corresponding liability at the date at which the leased asset       認為使用權資產，並確認相應負債。
      is available for use by the Group.

      Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially           租賃產生之資產及負債初步以現值進行
      measured on a present value basis. Lease liabilities include        計量。租賃負債包括以下租賃付款之淨
      the net present value of the following lease payments:              現值：

      •     fixed payments (including in-substance fixed                  ·   固定付款（包括實質固定付款）減
            payments), less any lease incentives receivable;                  任何應收租賃優惠；

      •     variable lease payment that are based on an index or          ·   基於指數或利率之可變租賃付款
            a rate, initially measured using the index or rate as at          （初 步 使 用 於 開 始 日 期 之 指 數 或
            the commencement date;                                            利率計量）  ；

      •     amounts expected to be payable by the Group under             ·   剩餘價值擔保下之本集團預期應
            residual value guarantees;                                        付款項；

      •     the exercise price of a purchase option if the Group is       ·   購買選擇權之行使價格（倘本集團
            reasonably certain to exercise that option; and                   合理地確定行使該選擇權）；及

      •     payments of penalties for terminating the lease, if the       ·   終止租賃所支付之罰款（倘租賃期
            lease term reflects the Group exercising that option.             反映本集團行使該選擇權）。

      The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate           租賃付款採用租賃所隱含之利率予以折
      implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be determined, the       現。倘 無 法 釐 定 該 利 率，則 使 用 承 租 人
      lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used, being the rate         之增量借款利率，即承租人在類似經濟
      that the lessee would have to pay to borrow the funds               環境中以類似條款及條件借入獲得類似
      necessary to obtain an asset of similar value in a similar          價值資產所需資金所必須支付的利率。
      economic environment with similar terms and conditions.

      Right-of-use assets are measured at cost comprising the             使用權資產按成本計量，包括以下各項：
      following:

      •     the amount of the initial measurement of lease liability;     ·   租賃負債之初步計量金額；

      •     any lease payments made at or before the                      ·   於開始日期或之前所作之任何租
            commencement date less any lease incentives                       賃 付 款，減 所 收 取 之 任 何 租 賃 優
            received;                                                         惠；

      •     any initial direct costs; and                                 ·   任何初始直接成本；及

      •     restoration costs.                                            ·   修復成本。

    Accounting policy for leases (Continued)                          租賃的會計政策（續）
    Right-of-use assets are generally depreciated over the            使用權資產一般於資產的可使用年期及
    shorter of the asset’s useful life and the lease term on          租 期（以 較 短 者 為 準）內 以 直 線 法 予 以
    a straight-line basis. If the Group is reasonably certain         折舊。倘本集團合理確定行使購買選擇
    to exercise a purchase option, the right-of-use asset is          權，則使用權資產於相關資產的可使用
    depreciated over the underlying asset’s useful life.              年期內予以折舊。

    Payments associated with short-term leases and leases of          與短期租賃及低價值資產租賃相關之付
    low-value assets are recognised on a straight-line basis as       款以直線法於綜合損益表中確認為開
    an expense in consolidated income statement. Short-term           支。短期租賃指租期為12個月或少於12
    leases are leases with a lease term of 12 months or less.         個月之租賃。低價值資產包括小型辦公
    Low-value assets comprise small items of office equipment.        室設備。

    (a)   Variable lease payments                                     (a)   可變租賃付款
          Estimation uncertainty arising from variable lease                可變租賃付款導致之估計不確定
          payments                                                          性
          Some property leases contain variable payment terms               部分物業租賃包含與店舖產生之
          that are linked to sales generated from a store. For              銷售相關聯之可變付款條款。對於
          individual stores, up to 100 percent of lease payments            個別店舖，基於可變付款條款之租
          are on the basis of variable payment terms and                    賃付款額最高可達100%，並且採
          there is a wide range of sales percentages applied.               用之銷售百分比範圍很廣。使用可
          Variable payment terms are used for a variety of                  變付款條款之原因有多種，包括使
          reasons, including minimising the fixed costs base                新設店舖之固定成本基數減至最
          for newly established stores. Variable lease payments             低。取決於銷售額之可變租賃付款
          that depend on sales are recognised in consolidated               在觸發付款之條件發生期間在綜
          income statement in the period in which the condition             合損益表中確認。
          that triggers those payments occurs.

    (b)   Extension and termination options                           (b)   續租及終止選擇權
          Extension and termination options are included in                 本集團之若干物業及設備租賃包
          certain property and equipment leases across the                  含續租及終止租賃之選擇權。該等
          Group. These terms are used to maximise operational               條款用於在管理合約方面最大限
          flexibility in terms of managing contracts. The                   度地提高操作靈活性。所擁有的大
          majority of extension and termination options held                部分續租及終止租賃選擇權僅可
          are exercisable only by the Group and not by the                  由本集團行使，而非由各自之出租
          respective lessor.                                                人行使。

    Lease income from operating leases where the Group is             本集團作為出租人的經營租賃的租賃收
    a lessor is recognised in income statement on a straight-         入以直線法於租期內於收益表內確認。
    line basis over the lease term. Initial direct costs incurred     獲取經營租賃產生的初始直接成本計入
    in obtaining an operating lease are added to the carrying         相關資產的賬面值，並於租期內以確認
    amount of the underlying asset and recognised as expenses         租賃收入的相同基準確認為開支。個別
    over the lease term on the same basis as lease income. The        租賃資產按其性質計入綜合資產負債
    respective leased assets are included in the consolidated         表。
    balance sheet based on their nature.

18 GOODWILL AND INTANGIBLE ASSETS                                          18 商譽及無形資產（續）
      (Continued)
      Accounting policies for goodwill and intangible                        商譽及無形資產及非金融資產減值
      assets and impairment of non-financial assets                          的會計政策
      (a)     Goodwill                                                       (a)   商譽
              Goodwill on acquisitions of subsidiaries is included                 收購附屬公司的商譽列入無形資
              in intangible assets. Goodwill is not amortised but it               產。商譽不予攤銷，惟每年進行減
              is tested for impairment annually, or more frequently                值測試，或當有事件出現或情況變
              if events or changes in circumstances indicate that                  動顯示可能出現減值時進行更頻
              it might be impaired, and is carried at cost less                    密減值測試，並按成本減去累計減
              accumulated impairment losses. Gains and losses on                   值虧損列示。出售一間實體的收益
              the disposal of an entity include the carrying amount                及虧損包括有關所售實體商譽的
              of goodwill relating to the entity sold.                             賬面金額。

      (b)     Trademarks                                                     (b)   商標
              Intangible assets other than goodwill, including                     商 譽 之 外 的 無 形 資 產（包 括 商 標）
              trademarks, are measured initially at cost or, if                    初始按成本計量，或倘為於業務合
              acquired in a business combination, fair value at the                併中收購，則按收購日期之公允值
              acquisition date. An intangible asset with a finite useful           計量。有限使用年期之無形資產於
              life is amortised on a straight-line basis over its useful           其使用年期內以直線法攤銷，並以
              life as follows and carried at cost less accumulated                 成本減累計攤銷及累計減值虧損
              amortisation and accumulated impairment losses.                      入賬。

              Trademarks                16.5 years                                 商標          16.5年

      (c)     Impairment of non-financial assets                             (c)   非金融資產減值
              Goodwill and intangible assets that have an indefinite               商譽及無特定可使用年期的無形
              useful life are not subject to amortisation and are                  資產毋需攤銷，並每年進行減值測
              tested annually for impairment, or more frequently if                試，或倘事件或情況有變反映可能
              events or changes in circumstances indicate that they                已 出 現 減 值，則 更 頻 繁 地 進 行 測
              might be impaired. Other non-financial assets are                    試。其他非金融資產則於事件或情
              tested for impairment whenever events or changes in                  況變動反映賬面值可能未能收回
              circumstances indicate that the carrying amount may                  時進行減值測試。減值虧損按資產
              not be recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised                 之賬面值超出可收回金額之差額
              for the amount by which the asset’s carrying amount                  確認。可收回金額以資產的公允值
              exceeds its recoverable amount. The recoverable                      減出售成本與使用價值兩者之較
              amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value less                   高者為準。為評估減值，資產將按
              costs of disposal and value-in-use. For the purposes                 獨立可識辨現金流入的最低層級
              of assessing impairment, assets are grouped at the                   分組，該等現金流入在很大程度上
              lowest levels for which there are separately identifiable            獨立於其他資產或資產組合（現金
              cash inflows which are largely independent of the cash               產生單位）的現金流入。於每個報
              inflows from other assets or groups of assets (cash-                 告期末，對發生減值的非金融資產
              generating units). Non-financial assets other than                   （除 商 譽 以 外）進 行 審 查，以 確 定
              goodwill that suffered an impairment are reviewed for                減值是否可能撥回。
              possible reversal of the impairment at the end of each
              reporting period.

30 EMPLOYEE BENEFIT OBLIGATIONS – LONG                                   30 僱員福利責任－長期服務金（續）
   SERVICE PAYMENT (Continued)
      Accounting policy for defined benefit obligation –                   定額福利責任－長期服務金的會計
      long service payment                                                 政策
      The Group’s net obligation in respect of long service                本集團就根據香港《僱傭條例》在若干情
      amounts payable on cessation of employment in certain                況下於僱員終止受僱時支付長期服務金
      circumstances under the Hong Kong Employment                         之責任淨額為有關僱員於本期間及過往
      Ordinance is the amount of future benefit that employees             期間以其所提供服務賺取之未來福利。
      have earned in return for their service in the current and prior
      periods.

      The long service payment liabilities are assessed by using           長期服務金負債採用認可精算師預計的
      the projected unit credit method by a qualified actuary.             單位貸記法評估。支付長期服務金負債
      The cost of providing the long service payment liabilities is        之成本會從綜合損益表中扣除，以便將
      charged to the consolidated profit and loss account so as to         成本於僱員之服務年期內攤分。
      spread the costs over the service lives of employees.

      The long service payment liabilities are discounted to               長期服務金負債通過折現釐定現值，並
      determine the present value and reduced by entitlements by           減去因本集團供款而於本集團定額供款
      employees accrued under the Group’s defined contribution             退休計劃下累計的僱員應享權利。減少
      retirement scheme that is attributable to contributions made         被視為與服務相關的僱員供款，因此，
      by the Group. The reductions are deemed as contributions             供款減少服務成本。本集團按直線法將
      from employees which are linked to service and hence,                僱員供款計入服務期。因經驗調整及精
      the contributions reduce service cost. The Group                     算假設變動而產生的精算損益分別計入
      attributes the contributions from employees to periods               或扣除自產生期間的其他全面收益。因
      of service on a straight-line basis. Actuarial gains and             計劃修訂或縮減而導致的定額福利義務
      losses arising from experience adjustments and changes               現值的變動立即於損益中確認為過去服
      in actuarial assumptions are credited or charged to other            務成本。
      comprehensive income in the period in which they arise,
      respectively. Changes in the present value of the defined
      benefit obligation resulting from plan amendments or
      curtailments are recognised immediately in profit or loss as
      past service costs.

      Accounting policy for gold loans                                                   黃金借貸的會計政策
      Gold loans to be repaid by cash are designated as financial                        將以現金償還的黃金借貸乃指定為按公
      liabilities at fair value through profit or loss as the gold loans                 允值計入損益之金融負債，原因為黃金
      form part of a contract containing one or more embedded                            借貸構成包含一項或多項嵌入式衍生工
      derivatives. Gold loans to be repaid by physical gold are                          具的合約的一部分。將以實物黃金償還
      classified as liabilities at fair value through profit or loss.                    的黃金借貸乃分類為按公允值計入損益
                                                                                         的負債。

      Gain or losses on gold loans are recognised in the                                 黃金借貸的收益或虧損計入綜合損益
      consolidated income statement. The net fair value gain or                          表。綜合損益表中確認的淨公允值收益
      loss recognised in the consolidated income statement does                          或虧損並不包括該等負債的任何利息。
      not include any interest charged on these liabilities.

      Gold loans due to be settled within twelve months after the                        報告期間後十二個月內到期償還的黃金
      reporting period are classified as current liabilities.                            借貸分類為流動負債。

              The contributed surplus of the Company represents the difference                        本公司之實繳盈餘指本公司為交換Luk Fook
              between the nominal value of the shares issued by the Company                           Investment (B.V.I.) Limited全部已發行普通股
              in exchange for all the issued ordinary shares of Luk Fook                              而發行股份之面值與本公司於1997年本集
              Investment (B.V.I.) Limited and the value of net assets of the                          團重組期間所收購相關附屬公司之資產淨
              underlying subsidiaries acquired by the Company during the                              值兩者之差額。根據百慕達《 1981年公司法 》
              Group’s reorganisation in 1997. Under the Companies Act 1981 of                         （經修訂），實繳盈餘可派發予股東，惟本公
              Bermuda (as amended), the contributed surplus is distributable to the                   司自實繳盈餘派付股息後，須能於負債到期
              shareholders, provided that the Company would, after the payment of                     時支付債務；或本公司之資產可變現價值據
              dividends out of the contributed surplus, be able to pay its liabilities                此不會少於其債務、其已發行股本及其股份
              as they become due; or the realisable value of the Company’s assets                     溢價之總和。就本集團層面而言，實繳盈餘
              would thereby not be less than the aggregate of its liabilities, its                    重新分類為各相關附屬公司之儲備部分。
              issued share capital and its share premium. At the Group level, the
              contributed surplus is reclassified into its component of reserves of
              the underlying subsidiaries.

        accounting (Continued)
        (b)   Subsidiaries                                                (b)   附屬公司
              Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured               附屬公司指本集團擁有控制
              entities) over which the Group has control. The                   權 的 所 有 實 體（包 括 結 構 性
              Group controls an entity when the Group is                        實體）。當本集團自參與實體
              exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns                    業務而承擔取得其可變回報
              from its involvement with the entity and has the                  的風險或享有可變回報的權
              ability to affect those returns through its power                 利，並 有 能 力 透 過 其 對 實 體
              to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries              活動的主導權影響該等回報
              are fully consolidated from the date on which                     時，則本集團控制該實體。附
              control is transferred to the Group. They are                     屬公司於控制權轉移至本集
              deconsolidated from the date that control                         團 當 日 起 全 面 合 併 入 賬，並
              ceases.                                                           於終止控制權當日起不再合
                                                                                併入賬。

              Intercompany transactions, balances and                           集 團 公 司 間 交 易、集 團 公 司
              unrealised gains on transactions between group                    間交易的結存及未變現收益
              companies are eliminated. Unrealised losses are                   均 會 對 銷。未 變 現 虧 損 亦 會
              also eliminated unless the transaction provides                   對 銷，除 非 該 交 易 提 供 證 據
              evidence of an impairment of the transferred                      證明所轉讓的資產出現減
              asset. Accounting policies of subsidiaries have                   值。附 屬 公 司 的 會 計 政 策 已
              been changed where necessary to ensure                            在 需 要 時 作 出 變 動，以 確 保
              consistency with the policies adopted by the                      與本集團所採納的政策一

      40.8 Trade and other receivables                                   40.8 貿易及其他應收賬項
           Trade receivables are amounts due from customers                貿易應收賬項為於正常業務過程
           for merchandise sold or services performed in the               中售出商品或提供服務之客戶結
           ordinary course of business. If collection of trade and         欠金額。如果貿易及其他應收賬項
           other receivables is expected in one year or less (or in        預計於一年或少於一年（或在業務
           the normal operating cycle of the business if longer),          正常經營週期內的更長時間）內收
           they are classified as current assets. If not, they are         回，則 被 分 類 為 流 動 資 產。否 則，
           presented as non-current assets.                                在非流動資產中呈列。

           Trade and other receivables are recognised initially            貿易及其他應收賬項初步按無條
           at the amount of consideration that is unconditional            件代價金額確認，除非當中包含重
           unless they contain significant financing components,           大融資成分時，則按公允值確認。
           when they are recognised at fair value. The Group               本集團以收取合約現金流量為目
           holds the trade and other receivables with the                  的 持 有 貿 易 及 其 他 應 收 賬 項，因
           objective of collecting the contractual cash flows and          此，貿易及其他應收賬項其後利用
           therefore measures them subsequently at amortised               實際利率法按攤銷成本減任何減
           cost using the effective interest method, less                  值撥備計量。
           allowance for impairment.

   40.9 Cash and cash equivalents                                       40.9 現金及現金等價物
        In the consolidated statement of cash flows, cash                   於綜合現金流量表中，現金及現金
        and cash equivalents include cash in hand, deposits                 等價物包括手頭現金、金融機構通
        held at call with financial institutions and other short-           知存款及原定於三個月或以內到
        term highly liquid investments with original maturities             期之其他短期高流通量投資，其可
        of three months or less that are readily convertible to             隨時轉換為已知金額現金且價值
        known amounts of cash and which are subject to an                   變動風險並不重大。
        insignificant risk of changes in value.

   40.10 Trade and other payables                                       40.10 貿易及其他應付賬項
        Trade payables are obligations to pay for goods or                  貿易應付賬項是於日常業務過程
        services that have been acquired in the ordinary                    中從供應商購買商品或接受服務
        course of business from suppliers. Trade and other                  產生之付款責任。倘貿易及其他應
        payable are classified as current liabilities if payment is         付賬項將在一年或少於一年內（若
        due within one year or less (or in the normal operating             更長則在業務正常經營週期內）支
        cycle of the business if longer). If not, they are                  付，則 歸 類 為 流 動 負 債。否 則，在
        presented as non-current liabilities.                               非流動負債中呈列。

        Trade and other payables are recognised initially at fair           貿易及其他應付賬項初步按公允
        value and subsequently measured at amortised cost                   值確認，其後以實際利率法按攤銷
        using the effective interest method.                                成本計量。

   40.11 Borrowings                                                     40.11 貸款
        Borrowings are carried initially at fair value, net                 貸款初步按公允值（扣除所產生交
        of transaction costs incurred. Borrowings are                       易成本）確認，其後按攤銷成本列
        subsequently stated at amortised cost; any difference               賬；所得款項（扣除交易成本）與贖
        between the proceeds (net of transaction costs) and                 回價值兩者間之任何差額，會以實
        the redemption value is recognised in the consolidated              際利率法於貸款期間內在綜合損
        income statement over the period of the borrowings                  益表中確認。
        using the effective interest method.

        Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are               為建立貸款融資所支付的費用，當
        recognised as transaction costs of the loan to the                  部分或所有融資很可能將被提取
        extent that it is probable that some or all of the facility         時確認為貸款的交易成本。於此情
        will be drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred               況下，該費用在提取貸款融資前予
        until the draw-down occurs. To the extent there is                  以遞延。倘無證據表明部分或所有
        no evidence that it is probable that some or all of the             融資很可能將被提取，則該費用將
        facility will be drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a            作為流動性服務的預付款項資本
        pre-payment for liquidity services and amortised over               化，並在融資相關之期限內攤銷。
        the period of the facility to which it relates.

      40.14 Dividend distribution                                          40.14 股息分派

           where appropriate.

           end of the reporting period but not distributed at the             情決定）作出撥備。
           end of the reporting period.

      40.15 Borrowing costs                                                40.15 貸款成本
           General and specific borrowing costs that are                      收購、建造或生產合資格資產直接
           directly attributable to the acquisition, construction             應佔的一般及特定貸款成本，乃於
           or production of a qualifying asset are capitalised                完成及準備該資產作其擬定用途
           during the period of time that is required to complete             或銷售的期間予以資本化。合資格
           and prepare the asset for its intended use or sale.                資產為須經一段相當時間以使其
           Qualifying assets are assets that necessarily take                 可作擬定用途或銷售的資產。
           a substantial period of time to get ready for their
           intended use or sale.

           Investment income earned on the temporary                          就有待用作合資格資產開支之特
           investment of specific borrowings pending their                    定貸款作暫時投資所賺取之投資
           expenditure on qualifying assets is deducted from the              收入乃自可予資本化之貸款成本
           borrowing costs eligible for capitalisation.                       中扣除。

           Other borrowing costs are expensed in the period in                其他貸款成本於產生期間支銷。
           which they are incurred.

      40.16 Offsetting financial instruments                               40.16 抵銷金融工具
           Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net            當具有抵銷已確認金額的法定可
           amount reported in the consolidated balance sheet                  執行權利且擬以淨額結算或同時
           when there is a legally enforceable right to offset                變現資產及清償負債時，金融資產
           the recognised amounts and there is an intention to                及負債將予抵銷，且淨額在綜合資
           settle on a net basis or realise the asset and settle              產負債表內呈列。合法可執行權利
           the liability simultaneously. The legally enforceable              不得取決於未來事件，且必須在正
           right must not be contingent on future events and                  常業務過程中以及在本集團或交
           must be enforceable in the normal course of business               易對手違約、無力償還債務或破產
           and in the event of default, insolvency or bankruptcy              的情況下可執行。本集團亦已訂立
           of the Group or the counterparty. The Group has                    不符合抵銷準則但仍允許在破產
           also entered into arrangements that do not meet                    或合約終止等若干情況下抵銷相
           the criteria for offsetting but still allow for the related        關金額的安排。
           amounts to be set off in certain circumstances, such
           as bankruptcy or the termination of a contract.
